That is the wrong shape\n/// for CLI consumers of `ready`/`list`/`search --format json`: a downstream\n/// filter that deserializes into a struct with optional `assignee`/`labels`\n/// fields cannot tell an omitted key from a genuinely unset value. We therefore\n/// normalize the display output so `assignee` is always emitted (`null` when\n/// unset) and `labels` is always an array (`[]` when empty).\nfn issue_to_value(issue: &Issue) -> Value {\n    let mut stripped = issue.clone();\n    stripped.dependencies = vec![];\n    stripped.comments = vec![];\n\n    let mut value = serde_json::to_value(&stripped).unwrap_or(Value::Null);\n    if let Value::Object(ref mut map) = value {\n        ensure_display_fields(map);\n    }\n    value\n}\n\n/// Guarantee the `assignee` and `labels` keys exist on a serialized issue map.\nfn ensure_display_fields(map: &mut Map<String, Value>) {\n    map.entry(\"assignee\")\n        .or_insert(Value::Null);\n    map.entry(\"labels\")\n        .or_insert_with(|| Value::Array(vec![]));\n}\n\nimpl Formatter for JsonFormatter {\n    fn format_issue(&self, issue: &Issue) -> String {\n        serde_json::to_string(&issue_to_value(issue)).unwrap_or_else(|_| \"{}\".to_string())\n    }\n\n    fn format_issues(&self, issues: &[Issue]) -> String {\n        issues\n            .iter()\n            .map(|issue| serde_json::to_string(&issue_to_value(issue)))\n            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()\n            .unwrap_or_default()\n            .join(\"\\n\")\n    }\n\n    fn format_error(&self, message: &str) -> String {\n        serde_json::json!({\"error\": message}).to_string()\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::model::Issue;\n    use serde_json::Value;\n\n    fn parse(line: &str) -> Value {\n        serde_json::from_str(line).expect(\"formatter must emit valid JSON\")\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn assignee_null_when_unset() {\n        let issue = Issue::new(\"Test\".to_string());\n        let v = parse(&JsonFormatter.format_issue(&issue));\n        assert_eq!(v.get(\"assignee\"), Some(&Value::Null));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn labels_empty_array_when_none() {\n        let issue = Issue::new(\"Test\".to_string());\n        let v = parse(&JsonFormatter.format_issue(&issue));\n        assert_eq!(v.get(\"labels\"), Some(&Value::Array(vec![])));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn assignee_and_labels_populated_when_present() {\n        let mut issue = Issue::new(\"Test\".to_string());\n        issue.assignee = Some(\"claude-code-glm-4.7-alpha\".to_string());\n        issue.labels = vec![\"split-child\".to_string()];\n        let v = parse(&JsonFormatter.format_issue(&issue));\n        assert_eq!(v.get(\"assignee\").and_then(|a| a.as_str()), Some(\"claude-code-glm-4.7-alpha\"));\n        assert_eq!(\n            v.get(\"labels\"),\n            Some(&Value::Array(vec![Value::String(\"split-child\".to_string())]))\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn format_issues_guarantees_fields_per_line() {\n        let a = Issue::new(\"A\".to_string());\n        let mut b = Issue::new(\"B\".to_string());\n        b.assignee = Some(\"worker\".to_string());\n        b.labels = vec![\"x\".to_string()];\n        let out = JsonFormatter.format_issues(&[a, b]);\n        for line in out.lines() {\n            let v = parse(line);\n            assert!(v.get(\"assignee\").is_some(), \"assignee key must be present\");\n {"parentUuid":"676d95c8-c635-429c-80c7-b4329983d7e5","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdH9hBZHknSLYqeoRfrgh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01D7cTZ721x3yNWkKxUK3Tyj","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/coding/bead-forge/tests/ready_json_fields.rs","content":"//! Regression tests for bf-1wj: `bf ready --format json` must always include\n//! `assignee` (null when unset) and `labels` ([] when empty), matching what\n//! `bf show` reports for the same bead.\n//!\n//! Root cause: the shared `JsonFormatter` serializes the `Issue` struct, whose\n//! `assignee`/`labels` fields carry `skip_serializing_if` (compact on-disk JSONL\n//! for br/bd compatibility). That omitted the keys entirely from CLI display\n//! output, silently breaking downstream consumers (e.g. a NEEDLE explore strand)\n//! that filter ready candidates on assignee/labels — an omitted key is\n//! indistinguishable from a genuinely unset value once deserialized.\n//!\n//! `ready` does NOT filter on assignee/labels (it selects on status='open'), so\n//! an open bead carrying a stale assignee and a label appears in the output WITH\n//! those fields populated.\n\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\nuse std::process::Command;\nuse tempfile::TempDir;\n\nfn bf_path() -> PathBuf {\n    std::env::var(\"CARGO_BIN_EXE_bf\")\n        .map(PathBuf::from)\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from(\"./target/debug/bf\"))\n}\n\nfn init_workspace() -> TempDir {\n    let temp_dir = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n    let out = Command::new(bf_path())\n        .args([\"init\", \"--prefix\", \"test\"])\n        .current_dir(temp_dir.path())\n        .output()\n        .expect(\"Failed to init workspace\");\n    assert!(\n        out.status.success(),\n        \"bf init failed: {}\",\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)\n    );\n    temp_dir\n}\n\nfn create_bead(workspace: &std::path::Path, title: &str, extra: &[&str]) -> String {\n    let mut cmd = Command::new(bf_path());\n    cmd.args([\"create\", \"--title\", title, \"--type\", \"task\", \"--priority\", \"2\"])\n        .args(extra)\n        .current_dir(workspace);\n    let out = cmd.output().expect(\"Failed to create bead\");\n    assert!(\n        out.status.success(),\n        \"bf create failed: {}\",\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)\n    );\n    String::from_utf8(out.stdout).unwrap().trim().to_string()\n}\n\n/// Parse `bf ready --format json` (JSONL, one object per line) into a map keyed\n/// by bead id.\nfn ready_json(workspace: &std::path::Path) -> std::collections::HashMap<String, serde_json::Value> {\n    let out = Command::new(bf_path())\n        .args([\"ready\", \"--limit\", \"0\", \"--format\", \"json\"])\n        .current_dir(workspace)\n        .output()\n        .expect(\"Failed to run bf ready\");\n    assert!(\n        out.status.success(),\n        \"bf ready failed: {}\",\n        String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)\n    );\n    let stdout = String::from_utf8(out.stdout).unwrap();\n    let mut map = std::collections::HashMap::new();\n    for line in stdout.lines() {\n        let line = line.trim();\n        if line.is_empty() || line == \"[]\" {\n            continue;\n        }\n        let v: serde_json::Value =\n            serde_json::from_str(line).unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!(\"invalid JSON line {line:?}: {e}\"));\n        if let Some(id) = v.get(\"id\").and_then(|i| i.as_str()) {\n            map.insert(id.to_string(), v);\n        }\n    }\n    map\n}\n\n/// A bead with a real assignee and a real label appears in `bf ready --json`\n/// WITH those fields populated (not omitted) — the exact scenario from bf-1wj.\n#[test]\nfn ready_json_includes_populated_assignee_and_labels() {\n    let ws = init_workspace();\n    let id = create_bead(\n        ws.path(),\n        \"assigned bead\",\n        &[\n            \"--assignee\",\n            \"claude-code-glm-4.7-alpha\",\n            \"--label\",\n            \"split-child\",\n        ],\n    );\n\n    let beads = ready_json(ws.path());\n    let bead = beads\n        .get(&id)\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {id} missing from ready output\"));\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        bead.get(\"assignee\").and_then(|a| a.as_str()),\n        Some(\"claude-code-glm-4.7-alpha\"),\n        \"assignee must be populated, got {:?}\",\n        bead.get(\"assignee\")\n    );\n    let labels = bead\n        .get(\"labels\")\n        .and_then(|l| l.as_array())\n        .expect(\"labels must be an array\");\n    assert!(\n        labels.iter().any(|l| l.as_str() == Some(\"split-child\")),\n        \"labels must contain split-child, got {labels:?}\"\n    );\n}\n\n/// A bead with no assignee and no labels still reports `assignee: null` and\n/// `labels: []` — the keys are always present so downstream consumers can\n/// deserialize them reliably.\n#[test]\nfn ready_json_emits_null_assignee_and_empty_labels_when_unset() {\n    let ws = init_workspace();\n    let id = create_bead(ws.path(), \"bare bead\", &[]);\n\n    let beads = ready_json(ws.path());\n    let bead = beads\n        .get(&id)\n        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"bead {id} missing from ready output\"));\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        bead.get(\"assignee\"),\n        Some(&serde_json::Value::Null),\n        \"assignee key must be present and null when unset\"\n    );\n    assert_eq!(\n        bead.get(\"labels\"),\n        Some(&serde_json::Value::Array(vec![])),\n        \"labels key must be present and an empty array when none\"\n    );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":598,"cache_read_input_tokens":71035,"output_tokens":2109,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":598,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":2109,"cache_read_input_tokens":71035,"cache_creation_input_tokens":598,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":598},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdH9hALcoxryQF2eHtjYz","type":"assistant","uuid":"cfe5a0d4-40b2-4e3a-b8e1-24aa6252db0c","timestamp":"2026-07-22T13:10:21.062Z","effort":"high","session_id":"d940d7c8-a2a3-436d-bdd3-7977894875a1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/coding/bead-forge","sessionId":"d940d7c8-a2a3-436d-bdd3-7977894875a1","version":"2.1.217","gitBranch":"main"}

That struct carries `#[serde(skip_serializing_if …)]` on `assignee` (skipped when `None`) and `labels` (skipped when empty) — intentional, to keep the on-disk JSONL compact and `br`/`bd`-compatible.
